## Module 06 — Trinity pillars and four bounded practices

dfa_shape: A complete table is a finite object with declared state and alphabet sets. This operation counts those sets and their transition entries after validating every destination. A missing transition is refused, because silently inventing a sink state would change the input contract. The count is about a supplied representation; it does not measure a physical system or establish a governing policy.

dfa_run: A word trace begins with the initial state and appends one state per supplied symbol. Acceptance depends on the final trace state belonging to the declared final set. The empty word therefore has a one-state trace. These records make transition order inspectable. They do not execute a real controller, impersonate an operator, or establish a participant outcome.

dfa_reachable: Reachability follows every declared outgoing edge from the initial state. Unreachable states remain visible in a separate list instead of being deleted. This matters in the singleton-ab fixture, which deliberately carries an unreachable accepting cycle. The operation can explain table structure without claiming that every state has been observed in a real workflow or that unreachable evidence should be erased.

dfa_live: Liveness here means only that a state can reach a declared accepting state. Reverse reachability starts with the finals and grows until no state can be added. This definition differs from fairness, responsiveness, health, safety, or a guarantee that a real process will finish. Keeping the definition explicit prevents the familiar word live from importing a stronger operational claim.

dfa_complement_membership: Complement membership negates acceptance only after the table is proved complete over the declared alphabet. A word outside that alphabet is refused rather than silently admitted to an undefined universe. Complementing a partial table would need a separate completion policy and fresh contract. The result is a finite-language calculation for a synthetic word, with no external decision or authority grant.

dfa_word_count: Dynamic counts advance one symbol-length layer at a time. Every transition contributes the number of words that reached its source; accepting-state counts are summed only at the requested length. Boolean values, negative lengths, fractions, and lengths beyond the declared bound are refused. Large exact integers remain counts of supplied formal words and are never treated as observed throughput, probability, or performance.

dfa_words: Shortlex enumeration orders words first by length and then by the sorted alphabet. The request carries a maximum length, and the implementation checks the total enumeration budget before generating candidates. Every result states its finite coverage explicitly. A list of accepted sample words does not establish unbounded equivalence, completeness of an external protocol, or the absence of longer counterexamples.

dfa_shortest: Breadth-first state search finds a shortest accepted word, using alphabet order to make ties deterministic. A null witness means the declared table accepts no word; an empty string means the initial state already accepts. Those are different outcomes. The search records only formal language structure and supplies no evidence about elapsed real time, operator effort, or optimal physical control.

dfa_finite: A language is infinite only when a reachable cycle can still reach acceptance. The implementation therefore intersects reachability and coaccessibility before detecting cycles. The deliberately unreachable accepting cycle must not make the singleton-ab language infinite. This is a bounded algorithm on a supplied complete table. It does not establish physical stability, a universal theorem beyond the model, or a safety certificate.

dfa_equivalent: Equivalence searches the complete reachable product of two supplied tables with the same alphabet. A state pair with different acceptance bits gives a counterexample word. Exhausting that finite product supports equality of the two declared languages. This is stronger than a bounded word sample, while remaining a statement about those exact tables and never about independently observed systems or real deployment behavior.

dfa_subset: Language inclusion uses the same finite product but looks specifically for a word accepted on the left and rejected on the right. Direction matters: swapping operands changes the question. The returned counterexample preserves that orientation. An inclusion result cannot transfer ownership, permissions, rights, or trust from one record to another; those are separate evidence and authority obligations.

dfa_disjoint: Disjointness asks whether any product state accepts on both sides. If the empty word is shared, the counterexample is the empty string rather than a missing value. Otherwise breadth-first search supplies the earliest shortlex shared word. Shared syntax is not evidence of shared identity, common provenance, consent, or independent corroboration; the operation has no authority to make those substitutions.

dfa_union_membership: Union membership reports the two individual acceptance bits and their Boolean disjunction for one supplied word. The output explicitly says that no new machine was constructed. This avoids conflating a pointwise result with a serialized product automaton. It remains useful for comparing a declared acceptance policy while making no decision for a real account, operator, participant, or affected person.

dfa_intersection_membership: Intersection membership reports conjunction for one word and preserves both operand results. A false intersection can arise because either or both operands reject. Those possibilities remain visible in the envelope. The calculation does not constitute agreement by two independent reviewers: the tables are synthetic, the owner is shared, and some comparison packages themselves share implementation lineage.

dfa_difference_membership: Difference membership preserves left-minus-right orientation. The result is true only when the left table accepts and the right table rejects. No source record is removed and no negative is erased by this operation. The term difference describes formal membership, not permission to delete artifacts, revoke an identity, alter a right, or rewrite an earlier receipt.

dfa_xor_membership: Symmetric difference asks whether exactly one operand accepts the word. Keeping both operand bits makes the result easy to audit without implying which language is preferable. A disagreement is a formal witness and not a quality score, professional judgment, scientific refutation, or authority decision. A later owner can use the witness to define a narrower comparison with explicit evidence requirements.

dfa_residual: A residual starts from the state reached after the declared prefix and then traces the suffix from that state. The full prefix-plus-suffix length remains bounded. The input table is preserved. This is a language derivative representation, not a claim that a task, identity, memory, or subjective state persists across a pause, tool invocation, restart, or change of model.

dfa_transition_text: Textual transition lines preserve the source label, symbol, and destination in a deterministic order. The report exposes initial and final states separately. This creates a structural alternative representation, but browser testing, assistive-technology evaluation, cognitive review, and affected-user acceptance remain reserved. The operation never claims accessibility conformance merely because its output is readable plain text.

dfa_provenance: The provenance envelope hashes the exact declared table in its stated UTF-8 LF JSON domain and compares that digest with supplied metadata. Missing labels and digests remain explicit gaps. A matching hash binds bytes; it cannot verify a real identity, an actual author, legal rights, independent review, trust governance, or a live credential lifecycle. Same-owner review remains named as such.

dfa_authority_reservation: Authority reservations name ten protected actions and record that none was executed. A requested execution is refused. Accepting the reservation record is not approval for the action it describes. Deployment, real control, real identity issuance, certification, legal and cultural decisions, Maori wording, complete privacy claims, empirical promotion, and Stage 20 all require their own exact evidence and competent authority.

Freed ID and CBR Heart remains priority. The four practice lenses are learning and design labels only. The optional next-owner practice is synthetic grammar-to-automaton comparison reviewer. No professional qualification or role is assigned to Caelen Morrow.

## Module 07 — Packages and point-in-time source evidence

Automata supplies deterministic finite-automaton APIs; Greenery supports regular-expression language manipulation; Interegular supports regex-overlap work and shares lineage with Greenery. Their primary documentation was checked at [Automata](https://caleb531.github.io/automata/api/fa/class-dfa/), [Greenery](https://github.com/qntm/greenery), and [Interegular](https://github.com/MegaIng/interegular). These sources supply software context only.

Three direct packages and four dependencies were installed only in the isolated D-first owner target after x1. All seven wheels matched their x1 sizes and SHA-256 values. There were 420 bounded binary-word comparisons and three adverse definition checks. The seven-query OSV snapshot returned no listed vulnerabilities at the recorded time. That snapshot is neither exhaustive security nor future safety.

## Module 08 — Ten skills and five family-current runners

The official Skill Creator initializer and quick validator were used. Each local and promoted interface exercised every advertised operation and refused unknown fields and out-of-scope operations. The 190 CLI predicates passed, and 46 promoted files matched their declared raw byte hashes. The ten completed skill instructions were read through EOF. No existing package was overwritten.
